|\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | GET /history/{code} returns a currency's rates over a period with exact minimum, maximum and mean. |\n| Depends On | DATABASE.md, FEATURE-WebFoundation.md |\n| Provides | GET /history/{code} |\n| Consumes | db.rates.history |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nReport one currency's stored rates between optional inclusive `start` and `end` dates, with statistics computed exactly from the stored text.\n\n## Routes\n\n| Route | Response 200 |\n|-------|--------------|\n| `GET /history/{code}[?start=<date>][&end=<date>]` | `{\"currency\", \"base\": \"EUR\", \"count\", \"min\", \"max\", \"mean\", \"points\": [{\"date\", \"rate\"}, ...]}` |\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Points come from `db.rates.history` ascending by date; both bounds are inclusive and optional.\n- `count` is the number of points.\n- `min` and `max` are the stored text of the smallest and largest rate by `Decimal` value, not by string order.\n- `mean` is the exact `Decimal` mean of the point rates, rounded half to even to six decimals and written with exactly six decimals, as a JSON string.\n- Point `rate` values are the stored text as JSON strings.\n- Empty ranges and unknown currencies are owned by later stories (FEATURE-HistoryEmpty.md, FEATURE-HistoryRejects.md).\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-RATE-004` — read: list a currency's history over a period - supplies: `currency`, `start_date`, `end_date` - returns: `date`, `rate`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC history-statistics Fix any seam that breaks this; no new interface is introduced.\n\n## Routes\n\n| Route | Role |\n|-------|------|\n| `GET /stats` | Counts and first/last dates reflect a load made after the app was created |\n| `GET /rates/latest` | Latest publication reflects a load made after the app was created |\n\n## Behavior\n\n- The web client is created once, before the load; a long-lived connection or cached result would hide the load and fail this feature.\n- Loading goes through the `load` command, not direct repository writes.\n- Reloading changed rates replaces them and the next request shows the new values.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by DATABASE.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC live-load-visible-in-stats ===\nIntent: A client created before a load sees the new dates and rates in /stats on the very next request, with counts that grow by exactly what the file contributed.\nRequires: executable=python3; |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | GET /history/{code} returns a currency's rates over a period with exact minimum, maximum and mean. |\n| Depends On | DATABASE.md, FEATURE-WebFoundation.md |\n| Provides | GET /history/{code} |\n| Consumes | db.rates.history |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nReport one currency's stored rates between optional inclusive `start` and `end` dates, with statistics computed exactly from the stored text.\n\n## Routes\n\n| Route | Response 200 |\n|-------|--------------|\n| `GET /history/{code}[?start=<date>][&end=<date>]` | `{\"currency\", \"base\": \"EUR\", \"count\", \"min\", \"max\", \"mean\", \"points\": [{\"date\", \"rate\"}, ...]}` |\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Points come from `db.rates.history` ascending by date; both bounds are inclusive and optional.\n- `count` is the number of points.\n- `min` and `max` are the stored text of the smallest and largest rate by `Decimal` value, not by string order.\n- `mean` is the exact `Decimal` mean of the point rates, rounded half to even to six decimals and written with exactly six decimals, as a JSON string.\n- Point `rate` values are the stored text as JSON strings.\n- Empty ranges and unknown currencies are owned by later stories (FEATURE-HistoryEmpty.md, FEATURE-HistoryRejects.md).\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-RATE-004` — read: list a currency's history over a period - supplies: `currency`, `start_date`, `end_date` - returns: `date`, `rate`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC history-statistics \"/history/{code}\"\nwith db_session() as db:\n    for date, rate in points:\n        db.rates.replace_rate(date=date, currency=code, rate=rate)\n    client = web_client()\n    resp = client.get(route.format(code=code))\n    assert resp.status_code == 200\n    body = resp.json()\n\nordered = sorted(points, key=lambda p: p[0])\nvalues = [Decimal(r) for _, r in points]\nmin_text = min(points, key=lambda p: Decimal(p[1]))[1]\nmax_text = max(points, key=lambda p: Decimal(p[1]))[1]\nmean = (sum(values) / len(values)).quantize(Decimal(\"0.000001\"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_EVEN)\n\nassert body[\"currency\"] == code\nassert body[\"base\"] == \"EUR\"\nassert body[\"count\"] == len(points)\nassert body[\"min\"] == min_text\nassert body[\"max\"] == or a numeric zero for `min`, `max` or `mean`.\n- No floating point is introduced for the empty case.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-HistoryRejects.md ===\n# FEATURE: History Rejects\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | GET /history/{code} answers 404 for a currency with no stored rate at all and 422 for a malformed start or end date. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-HistoryEmpty.md |\n| Provides | GET /history/{code} rejects |\n| Consumes | GET /history/{code} empty |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nDistinguish a currency the store has never held, or EUR, which is never stored, from a known currency with an empty range, and refuse malformed dates.\n\n## Routes\n\n| Route | Condition | Status |\n|-------|-----------|--------|\n| `GET /history/{code}` | The currency has no stored rate at all, including `EUR`, regardless of range | 404 |\n| `GET /history/{code}` | `start` or `end` is not a real `YYYY-MM-DD` date | 422 |\n\n## Behavior\n\n- The 404 test considers all stored dates, not the requested range; a known currency with an empty range is 200 (FEATURE-HistoryEmpty.md).\n- Date validation is FastAPI request validation.\n- Error response bodies are not part of the contract.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by DATABASE.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC history-unknown-currency ===\nIntent: A currency with no stored rate returns 404 with or without a range, and EUR returns 404 even while other currencies are stored.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\nroute = \"/history/{code}\"\nunknown = \"ZZQ\"\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=\"1800-01-10\", currency=\"ZZA\", rate=\"1.5\")\n    client = web_client()\n    for code in [unknown, \"EUR\"]:\n        resp = client.get(route.format(code=code))\n        assert resp.status_code == 404, code\n        resp = client.get(\n            route.format(code=code), params={\"start\": \"1800-01-01\", \"end\": \"1800-12-31\"}\n        )\n        assert resp.status_code == 404, code\n=== END AC history-unknown-currency ===\n\n=== AC history-known-currency-out-of-range ===\nIntent: A currency with stored rates is not a 404 when the range is empty, so the 404 depends on the whole store and not the range.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\n\ncode = \"ZZK\"\nroute = \"/history/{code}\"\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=\"1800-02-10\", currency=code, rate=\"1.5\")\n    client = web_client()\n    resp = ===\n\n=== AC history-malformed-dates ===\nIntent: Malformed or impossible start and end dates on a stored currency are rejected with 422.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: \"/history/{code}\"\nbad_values = [\"2025-01-31-x\", \"2025-13-01\", \"2025-02-30\", \"20250131\", \"today\"]\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.rates.replace_rate(date=\"1800-03-10\", currency=code, rate=\"1.5\")\n    client = web_client()\n    for bad in bad_values:\n        resp = client.get(route.format(code=code), params={\"start\": bad})\n        assert resp.status_code == 422, (\"start\", client.get(route.format(code=code), params={\"start\": \"1800-01-01\", \"end\": bad})\n        assert resp.status_code == 422, (\"both\", bad, resp.status_code)\n=== END AC history-malformed-dates validation for 422.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-LiveVisibility.md ===\n# FEATURE: Live Visibility\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261027 V1 |\n| Description | Integration proof that a load completed while the application is running is visible to the next request. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-Serve.md, FEATURE-Stats.md, FEATURE-LoadSummary.md |\n| Provides | live load visibility |\n| Consumes | GET /stats, fxrates load, fxrates serve |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nAssembly and intent: each request opens its own database handle through `persistence.open_database`, so a load committed after the app was created is reflected by the next `/stats` and `/rates/latest` request. Fix any seam that breaks this; no new interface is introduced.\n\n## Routes\n\n| Route | Role |\n|-------|------|\n| `GET /stats` | Counts and first/last dates reflect a load made after the app was created |\n| `GET /rates/latest` | Latest publication reflects a load made after the app was created |\n\n## Behavior\n\n- The web client is created once, before the load; a long-lived connection or cached result would hide the load and fail this feature.\n- Loading goes through the `load` command, not direct repository writes.\n- Reloading changed rates replaces them and the next request shows the new values.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by DATABASE.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC live-load-visible-in-stats ===\nIntent: A client created before a load sees the new dates and rates in /stats on the very next request, with counts that grow by exactly what the file contributed.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\ndates = [\"9998-12-30\", \"9998-12-31\"]\ncurrencies = [\"ZZA\", \"ZZB\"]\nrates = {(d, c): f\"{i + 1}.{j + 1}5\" for i, d in enumerate(dates) for j, c in enumerate(currencies)}\n\nclient = web_client()\nbefore_resp = client.get(\"/stats\")\nassert before_resp.status_code == 200\nbefore = before_resp.json()\nassert before[\"last\"] != dates[-1]\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    path = os.path.join(tmp, \"live.csv\")\n    with open(path, \"w\", newline=\"\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as fh:\n        writer = csv.writer(fh, lineterminator=\"\\n\")\n        writer.writerow([\"Date\", *currencies, \"\"])\n        for d in dates:\n            writer.writerow([d, *[rates[(d, c)] for c in currencies], \"\"])\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"load\", path)\n    assert code == 0, err\n\nafter_resp = client.get(\"/stats\")\nassert after_resp.status_code == 200\nafter = after_resp.json()\nassert after[\"dates\"] == before[\"dates\"] + len(dates)\nassert after[\"rates\"] == before[\"rates\"] + len(rates)\nassert after[\"last\"] == max(dates)\n=== END AC live-load-visible-in-stats ===\n\n=== AC live-load-visible-in-rates ===\nIntent: A load made after the client was created shows up as the latest publication with the loaded rate text, and the request before the load did not.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\ndate = \"9998-12-29\"\ncurrency = \"ZZL\"\nrate = \"3.1400\"\n\nclient = web_client()\nbefore = client.get(\"/rates/latest\")\nassert before.status_code in (200, 404)\nif before.status_code == 200:\n    assert before.json()[\"date\"] != date\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    path = os.path.join(tmp, \"live.csv\")\n    with open(path, \"w\", newline=\"\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as fh:\n        writer = csv.writer(fh, lineterminator=\"\\n\")\n        writer.writerow([\"Date\", currency, \"\"])\n        writer.writerow([date, rate, \"\"])\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"load\", path)\n    assert code == 0, err\n\nafter = client.get(\"/rates/latest\")\nassert after.status_code == 200\nbody = after.json()\nassert body[\"date\"] == date\nassert body[\"rates\"][currency] == rate\n=== END AC live-load-visible-in-rates ===\n\n=== AC live-reload-replaces-visible ===\nIntent: Reloading a file with a changed rate replaces the stored value and the next request returns the new text without changing the date count.\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=fastapi; scope=test\nRequires: python-package=httpx2; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom app_server.testing import web_client\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\ndate = \"9998-12-28\"\ncurrency = \"ZZR\"\nfirst, second = \"1.5\", \"2.25\"\n\ndef write_and_load(path, rate):\n    with open(path, \"w\", newline=\"\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as fh:\n        writer = csv.writer(fh, lineterminator=\"\\n\")\n        writer.writerow([\"Date\", currency, \"\"])\n        writer.writerow([date, rate, \"\"])\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"load\", path)\n    assert code == 0, err\n\nclient = web_client()\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    path = os.path.join(tmp, \"live.csv\")\n    write_and_load(path, first)\n    mid = client.get(\"/stats\")\n    assert mid.status_code == 200\n    mid_body = mid.json()\n    seen = client.get(\"/rates/\" + date)\n    assert seen.status_code == 200\n    assert seen.json()[\"rates\"][currency] == first\n\n    write_and_load(path, second)\n    end = client.get(\"/stats\")\n    assert end.status_code == 200\n    end_body = end.json()\n    assert end_body[\"dates\"] == mid_body[\"dates\"]\n    assert end_body[\"rates\"] == mid_body[\"rates\"]\n    replaced = client.get(\"/rates/\" + date)\n    assert replaced.status_code == 200\n    assert replaced.json()[\"rates\"][currency] == second\n=== END AC live-reload-replaces-visible ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- The app never caches rates or holds a long-lived database handle across requests.\n- Application startup never initializes the schema; only `fxrates init` does.\n- Acceptance loads through the `load` command interface, never by writing rows directly.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===","ttft_ms":34178,"type":"result","duration_ms":90821,"uuid":"419b4eac-c16d-4e43-b7c1-79666b144556","ttft_stream_ms":5077,"time_to_request_ms":422,"first_content_frame_ms":5078,"queued_turn_count":0,"result_index":0,"model_usage_since_last_result":{"claude-sonnet-5-5":{"inputTokens":2,"outputTokens":14412,"cacheReadInputTokens":1545,"cacheCreationInputTokens":93769,"webSearchRequests":0,"costUSD":0.5193545,"contextWindow":1000000,"maxOutputTokens":128000,"thinkingTokens":3583,"cacheCreationEphemeral5mInputTokens":0,"cacheCreationEphemeral1hInputTokens":93769,"canonicalModel":"claude-sonnet-5-5","provider":"firstParty","costBasis":"list"}}}
